How to use this knowledge base when answering questions about Hypixel SkyBlock Wiki:
- Answer from this content only. If it is not covered, say so; never guess numbers.
- Find relevant pages in index.md by title, aliases (aka) and categories.
- [[X]] links to page X; [[X|y]] and 'y ([[X]])' are text y linking to X. Follow links and each page's 'Linked from' list to gather related facts (e.g. where an item drops or is used).
- Cite the [[Page]] each fact comes from. Reply in the user's language.

### Tasks Available
|Name|Requirement|
|-|-|
[Personal Goals]
|Rock Solid|Equip a Common or higher tiered Rock Pet.|
|Gunpowder Collector|Reach 55,000 Gunpowder Collection.|
|Strongman|Reach 120 [[Strength]].|
|Carry-On|Craft a Large Backpack.|
|Sightseer|Visit 7 different public islands.|
|Clay Ball Collector|Reach 150,000 Clay Ball Collection.|
|Skill Average|Reach a non-cosmetic Skill Average of 10.|
|Pair Up!|Find 5 Pairs in the Experimentation Table.|
|Cheetah|Obtain a Cheetah Talisman.|
|Warrior|Obtain level 10 in the Combat Skill.|
|Cocoa Beans Collector|Reach 185,000 Cocoa Beans Collection.|
|Commission Master|Complete 4 Commissions in the Dwarven Mines, Crystal Hollows, or Glacite Tunnels.|
|A Lost Cause|Kill a Lost Adventurer.|
|Powder Collector|Collect 50,000 Mithril Powder.|
|Love Is All Around|Obtain a Mediocre Ring of Love.|
|Trouble is Brewing...|Brew any level IV or higher potion.|
|Fashionable|Wear 7 unique Armor Sets.|
|Podium Position|Obtain a Silver or higher Medal in Jacob's Farming Contest.|
|Titanic Reforger|Apply the Titanic reforge to an item.|
|Divan's Chance|Place all 5 crystals in the Nucleus in the Crystal Hollows.|
[Community Goals]
|Operation: Ore|Break 10M Ore Blocks.|
|Tarantula Broodfather|Kill 10,000 Tarantula Broodfather of tier II or higher.|
|KABOOM!|Blow up 75k Crypts in The Catacombs.|
|Scarf Offensive|Defeat Scarf 4,000 times.|
|Lucky Looter|Loot 250k drops with a chance of 1% or lower|
